			<script type="text/javascript">
				const page_loaded_time = new Date().valueOf();

				window.addEventListener('error', function (evt) {
					evt.preventDefault();

					let err_stack = null;

					if(typeof evt.error != 'undefined' && evt.error != null){
						err_stack = evt.error.stack.split("\n").map(function(err){ return err.trim() })
					} else {
						err_stack = ['evt.error was undefined or null', evt];
					}

					let page_loaded_secs = (new Date().valueOf() - page_loaded_time) / 1000;

					let error_log_info = {
						page: window.location.href,
						msg: evt.message,
						file: evt.filename,
						line: evt.lineno,
						col: evt.colno,
						err_stack: err_stack,
						user_info: user_info,
						browser: window.navigator.userAgent,
						timestamp: Date.now(),
					}

					var formData = new FormData();
					formData.append("error_info", JSON.stringify(error_log_info));
					var req = new XMLHttpRequest();
					req.open("POST", "/ajax/log-error.php");
					req.send(formData);

					console.log(evt); 
				});

			</script>

<script type="text/javascript">


	(function($) {

		$(function () { //jquery ready function

			$(".off-canvas-submenu").hide();
			$(document).on('click', '.off-canvas-submenu-call', function(){
				$(this).parent().next(".off-canvas-submenu").slideToggle('fast');
			})
			
		    $(document).on('click', '.dark-mode-switch', function(){
		        let is_darkmode = $('input', $(this)).is(":checked")
		        document.cookie = "dark_mode="+is_darkmode+"; domain=fantasycruncher.com; expires=Fri, 31 Dec 9999 23:59:59 GMT; path=/"
		        if(is_darkmode){
		            $('body').addClass('dark')
		        } else {
		            $('body').removeClass('dark')
		        }
		    })
		    .on('mouseover', '.main-nav-links li', function(){
				var mm = $(this).find('.nav-mega-menu');
				var maxLeft = $(this).offset().left;
				var ww = $(window).width();

				mm.css({'left':'0'});			
				var mmWidth = mm.width();
				left = (ww-mmWidth < maxLeft) ? ww-mm.width() : maxLeft;
				mm.css({'left':'calc('+left+'px - 2vw)'});	
			})
			.on('click', '.mobile-header-logo-link', function(e){
				//this slows down the accidental clicking on the logo on mobile
			    var unix_timestamp = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)
			    if(typeof $(this).attr('last-clicked') != 'undefined'){
			       if( (unix_timestamp - $(this).attr('last-clicked')) < 3 ) window.location.assign("/")
			    }

			    $(this).attr('last-clicked', unix_timestamp)		            
			})
			.on('click', '.offseason-sports-handle', function(e){
				if($('.offseason-sports-container').hasClass('open')){
					$('.offseason-sports-container').removeClass('open')
					$(this).attr('title', 'Show Off Season Sports')
				} else {
					$('.offseason-sports-container').addClass('open')
					$(this).attr('title', 'Hide Off Season Sports')
				}
				
			})

		})

		
	})( jQuery );

	//this will load all the images once the DOMContent is loaded
	// window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', (event) => {
	//     document.querySelectorAll('.main-nav-links a.mega-menu-insights-article-link img[data-src]').forEach(img => {
	// 	    img.src = img.dataset.src
	// 	    delete img.dataset.src
	// 	})
	// });

	//this loads all the images the 1st time the mouseenters the main nav
	document.querySelector('.main-nav-links').addEventListener('mouseenter', (e) => {
		e.target.querySelectorAll('a.mega-menu-insights-article-link img[data-src]').forEach(img => {
		    img.src = img.dataset.src
		    delete img.dataset.src
		})
	}, { once: true })

	//this loads each sport individually when the mouse enters that sport
	// document.querySelector('.main-nav-links').addEventListener('mouseover', (e) => {
	// 	const targetElem = e.target
	// 	const mainNavListItem = targetElem.closest('li.main-nav-list-item')
	// 	if(mainNavListItem != null){
	// 		mainNavListItem.querySelectorAll('a.mega-menu-insights-article-link img[data-src]').forEach(img => {
	// 		    img.src = img.dataset.src
	// 		    delete img.dataset.src
	// 		})
	// 	}	
	// })

</script>

                        <div style="margin-bottom:50px">
                              <h4>ABOUT</h4>
                              <p>
                                FantasyCruncher is a set of tools that were developed for DFS players by DFS players. If you are serious about playing Daily Fantasy, this is the place to be, and you will be in the company of today's top DFS players.
                              </p>
                              <p>
                                Development of the software started in 2014 and since then has gone on to change the DFS world forever. While others have attempted to replicate, no other software service can come close to offering the multitude of features, the sports & DFS sites supported, the ease of use, the consistent updates, or the customer service we offer.
                              </p>
                              <p>
                                We are driven by our customers and listen when new features or additions are requested. We have a constant development cycle and will continue to improve and update the software we offer making sure you always have the biggest edge possible playing DFS.
                              </p>
                              <p>
                                Whether you play on <a href="https://www.fanduel.com/" target="blank">FanDuel</a>, <a href="https://www.draftkings.com/" target="blank">DraftKings</a>, <a href="https://sports.yahoo.com/dailyfantasy" target="blank">Yahoo</a> or <a href="https://ownersbox.com/" target="blank">OwnersBox</a> we have you covered and support all the sports including; NFL, NBA, MLB, NHL, College Football, College Basketball, GOLF, MMA, NASCAR, SOCCER, WNBA, eSPORTS (League of Legends, Call of Duty, Counter Strike, and Valorant), UFL, CFL, Tennis, F1 and the list keeps growing.
                              </p>
